TECHNICAL_STORAGE_KEY = "albion_technical_window_size"

def _restore_and_watch_size():
    """
    Восстанавливает размер технического окна из localStorage
    и запоминает последующие изменения размера.
    """

    ui.run_javascript(
        f"""
        setTimeout(() => {{
            const card = document.getElementById('technical-dialog-card');

            if (!card) {{
                return;
            }}

            const storageKey = '{TECHNICAL_STORAGE_KEY}';

            // ------------------------------------------------
            // RESTORE
            // ------------------------------------------------

            try {{
                const saved = localStorage.getItem(storageKey);

                if (saved) {{
                    const size = JSON.parse(saved);

                    if (size.width) {{
                        card.style.width = size.width + 'px';
                    }}

                    if (size.height) {{
                        card.style.height = size.height + 'px';
                    }}
                }}
            }} catch (error) {{
                console.warn(
                    'Не удалось восстановить размер технического окна',
                    error
                );
            }}

            // ------------------------------------------------
            // WATCH RESIZE
            // ------------------------------------------------

            if (card.__albionResizeObserver) {{
                card.__albionResizeObserver.disconnect();
            }}

            card.__albionResizeObserver = new ResizeObserver(() => {{
                try {{
                    localStorage.setItem(
                        storageKey,
                        JSON.stringify({{
                            width: Math.round(card.offsetWidth),
                            height: Math.round(card.offsetHeight)
                        }})
                    );
                }} catch (error) {{
                    console.warn(
                        'Не удалось сохранить размер технического окна',
                        error
                    );
                }}
            }});

            card.__albionResizeObserver.observe(card);

        }}, 100);
        """
    )

def show_technical(initial_tab="photo_processing"):

    with ui.dialog() as dialog:

        with ui.card().props(
            "id=technical-dialog-card"
        ).classes(
            "technical-dialog-card"
        ).style(
            """
            width: 1000px;
            height: 760px;
            min-width: 700px;
            min-height: 500px;
            max-width: 96vw;
            max-height: 94vh;
            resize: both;
            overflow: hidden;
            """
        ):

            # ====================================================
            # HEADER
            # ====================================================

            with ui.row().classes(
                "w-full items-center justify-between"
            ):

                ui.label(
                    "⚙ Технический раздел"
                ).classes(
                    "text-xl font-bold"
                )

                ui.button(
                    icon="close",
                    on_click=dialog.close,
                ).props(
                    "flat"
                )

            ui.separator()

            # ====================================================
            # TABS
            # ====================================================

            with ui.tabs().classes(
                "w-full"
            ) as tabs:

                ui.tab(
                    "photo_processing",
                    label="Обработка фото",
                    icon="photo_library",
                )

                ui.tab(
                    "last_run",
                    label="Последний запуск",
                    icon="history",
                )

                ui.tab(
                    "automation",
                    label="Автоматизация",
                    icon="autorenew",
                )

                ui.tab(
                    "qwen",
                    label="Qwen",
                    icon="smart_toy",
                )

                ui.tab(
                    "data",
                    label="Данные",
                    icon="storage",
                )

                ui.tab(
                    "system",
                    label="Система",
                    icon="computer",
                )

                ui.tab(
                    "fingerprints",
                    label="Fingerprints",
                    icon="fingerprint",
                )

                ui.tab(
                    "users",
                    label="Пользователи",
                    icon="people",
                )

            ui.separator()

            # ====================================================
            # TAB CONTENT
            # ====================================================

            with ui.scroll_area().classes(
                "w-full flex-1"
            ):

                with ui.tab_panels(
                    tabs,
                    value="photo_processing",
                ).classes(
                    "w-full"
                ):

                    with ui.tab_panel("photo_processing"):
                        build_photo_processing()

                    with ui.tab_panel("last_run"):
                        build_last_run()

                    with ui.tab_panel("automation"):
                        build_automation()

                    with ui.tab_panel("qwen"):
                        build_qwen()

                    with ui.tab_panel("data"):
                        build_data()

                    with ui.tab_panel("system"):
                        build_system()

                    with ui.tab_panel("fingerprints"):
                        build_fingerprints()

                    with ui.tab_panel("users"):
                        build_users()

    dialog.open()

    # Открываем нужную вкладку сразу.
    if initial_tab:
        ui.timer(
            0.05,

Сделаем сразу полноценную вкладку:

- список активных пользователей;
- текущий пользователь;
- 32 готовых цвета;
- произвольный цвет;
- сохранение цвета в SQLite;

```bash
cd ~/projects/scene_fingerprint_mvp_v02 && \

from nicegui import ui

from app.web.state import STATE
from app.web.users_db import (
    get_users,
    add_user,
    update_user_color,
    archive_user,
    restore_user,
)

# ============================================================
# PALETTE — 32 COLORS
# ============================================================

USER_COLORS = [
    "#E53935",
    "#D81B60",
    "#8E24AA",
    "#5E35B1",
    "#3949AB",
    "#1E88E5",
    "#039BE5",
    "#00ACC1",
    "#00897B",
    "#43A047",
    "#7CB342",
    "#C0CA33",
    "#FDD835",
    "#FFB300",
    "#FB8C00",
    "#F4511E",
    "#6D4C41",
    "#757575",
    "#546E7A",
    "#263238",
    "#AD1457",
    "#6A1B9A",
    "#283593",
    "#1565C0",
    "#00695C",
    "#2E7D32",
    "#558B2F",
    "#9E9D24",
    "#EF6C00",
    "#BF360C",
    "#4E342E",
    "#37474F",
]

def build_users():

    selected_color = {
        "value": "#1976D2"
    }

    show_archived = {
        "value": False
    }

    # ========================================================
    # MAIN CONTAINER
    # ========================================================

    root = ui.column().classes(
        "w-full gap-4 p-3"
    )

    # ========================================================
    # TITLE
    # ========================================================

    with root:

        ui.label(
            "Пользователи"
        ).classes(
            "text-xl font-bold"
        )

        ui.label(
            "Управление пользователями и цветом интерфейса"
        ).classes(
            "text-sm text-grey-6"
        )

        ui.separator()

        user_list = ui.column().classes(
            "w-full gap-2"
        )

        # ====================================================
        # ADD USER
        # ====================================================

        def add_user_dialog():

            with ui.dialog() as dialog:

                with ui.card().classes(
                    "w-[440px] max-w-[92vw]"
                ):

                    ui.label(
                        "Добавить пользователя"
                    ).classes(
                        "text-lg font-bold"
                    )

                    name_input = ui.input(
                        "Имя пользователя"
                    ).classes(
                        "w-full"
                    )

                    color_state = {
                        "value": USER_COLORS[5]
                    }

                    color_preview = ui.element(
                        "div"
                    ).classes(
                        "w-10 h-10 rounded"
                    ).style(
                        f"background: {color_state['value']};"
                    )

                    ui.label(
                        "Цвет"
                    ).classes(
                        "text-sm font-medium"
                    )

                    with ui.row().classes(
                        "flex-wrap gap-1"
                    ):

                        for color in USER_COLORS:

                            ui.button(
                                "",
                                on_click=lambda e,
                                c=color:
                                    choose_add_color(
                                        c,
                                        color_state,
                                        color_preview,
                                    ),
                            ).props(
                                "round"
                            ).style(
                                f"""
                                background: {color} !important;
                                width: 28px;
                                height: 28px;
                                min-width: 28px;
                                """
                            )

                    custom_color = ui.color_input(
                        value=color_state["value"]
                    ).classes(
                        "w-full"
                    )

                    def save():

                        name = (
                            name_input.value
                            or ""
                        ).strip()

                        color = (
                            custom_color.value
                            or color_state["value"]
                        )

                        if not name:

                            ui.notify(
                                "Введите имя пользователя",
                                type="warning",
                            )

                            return

                        if not add_user(
                            name,
                            color,
                        ):

                            ui.notify(
                                "Такой пользователь уже существует",
                                type="warning",
                            )

                            return

                        dialog.close()
                        rebuild()

                    with ui.row().classes(
                        "w-full justify-end gap-2 mt-3"
                    ):

                        ui.button(
                            "Отмена",
                            on_click=dialog.close,
                        ).props(
                            "flat"
                        )

                        ui.button(
                            "Добавить",
                            icon="person_add",
                            on_click=save,
                        )

            dialog.open()

        # ====================================================
        # COLOR HELPERS
        # ====================================================

        def choose_add_color(
            color,
            state,
            preview,
        ):

            state["value"] = color

            preview.style(
                f"background: {color};"
            )

        def choose_existing_color(
            user_id,
            color,
        ):

            update_user_color(
                user_id,
                color,
            )

            if STATE.user_id == user_id:

                print(
                    "[USER COLOR CHANGED]",
                    user_id,
                    color,
                    flush=True,
                )

            rebuild()

        # ====================================================
        # ARCHIVE / RESTORE
        # ====================================================

        def toggle_archive(
            user_id,
            active,
        ):

            if active:

                if STATE.user_id == user_id:

                    ui.notify(
                        "Нельзя архивировать текущего пользователя",
                        type="warning",
                    )

                    return

                archive_user(
                    user_id
                )

            else:

                restore_user(
                    user_id
                )

            rebuild()

        # ====================================================
        # REBUILD
        # ====================================================

        def rebuild():

            user_list.clear()

            with user_list:

                users = get_users(
                    active_only=not show_archived["value"]
                )

                if not users:

                    ui.label(
                        "Нет пользователей"
                    ).classes(
                        "text-grey-6 p-4"
                    )

                    return

                for (
                    user_id,
                    name,
                    color,
                    active,
                ) in users:

                    is_current = (
                        STATE.user_id == user_id
                    )

                    with ui.card().classes(
                        "w-full"
                    ):

                        with ui.row().classes(
                            "w-full items-center gap-3"
                        ):

                            # --------------------------------
                            # COLOR INDICATOR
                            # --------------------------------

                            ui.element(
                                "div"
                            ).style(
                                f"""
                                width: 18px;
                                height: 42px;
                                min-width: 18px;
                                border-radius: 4px;
                                background: {color};
                                """
                            )

                            # --------------------------------
                            # NAME
                            # --------------------------------

                            with ui.column().classes(
                                "flex-grow gap-0"
                            ):

                                ui.label(
                                    name
                                ).classes(
                                    "font-medium"
                                )

                                if is_current:

                                    ui.label(
                                        "Текущий пользователь"
                                    ).classes(
                                        "text-xs text-primary"
                                    )

                                if not active:

                                    ui.label(
                                        "Архив"
                                    ).classes(
                                        "text-xs text-grey-6"
                                    )

                            # --------------------------------
                            # ARCHIVE / RESTORE
                            # --------------------------------

                            ui.button(
                                icon=(
                                    "archive"
                                    if active
                                    else "unarchive"
                                ),
                                on_click=lambda e,
                                uid=user_id,
                                a=active:
                                    toggle_archive(
                                        uid,
                                        a,
                                    ),
                            ).props(
                                "flat round"
                            )

                        # ------------------------------------
                        # COLOR PALETTE
                        # ------------------------------------

                        if active:

                            with ui.row().classes(
                                "flex-wrap gap-1 mt-2"
                            ):

                                for palette_color in USER_COLORS:

                                    ui.button(
                                        "",
                                        on_click=lambda e,
                                        uid=user_id,
                                        c=palette_color:
                                            choose_existing_color(
                                                uid,
                                                c,
                                            ),
                                    ).props(
                                        "round"
                                    ).style(
                                        f"""
                                        background:
                                            {palette_color}
                                            !important;
                                        width: 25px;
                                        height: 25px;
                                        min-width: 25px;
                                        """
                                    )

                            # --------------------------------
                            # CUSTOM COLOR
                            # --------------------------------

                            ui.color_input(
                                value=color,
                                on_change=lambda e,
                                uid=user_id:
                                    choose_existing_color(
                                        uid,
                                        e.value,
                                    ),
                            ).classes(
                                "w-full mt-1"
                            )

        # ====================================================
        # TOOLBAR
        # ====================================================

        with ui.row().classes(
            "w-full items-center justify-between"
        ):

            ui.button(
                "Добавить пользователя",
                icon="person_add",
                on_click=add_user_dialog,
            ).props(
                "unelevated"
            )

            ui.checkbox(
                "Показывать архивных",
                value=False,
                on_change=lambda e:
                    show_archived.update(
                        value=e.value
                    ) or rebuild(),
            )

        rebuild()
PY
```

Да. И это будет лучше: **в техническом разделе → Пользователи** показываем для каждого пользователя:

- имя;
- **один цветовой кружок** — цвет пользователя;
- палитру из **10 предзаданных цветов**, по одному на пользователя;
- отдельный кружок **«Произвольный цвет»** с пипеткой;
- выбор цвета сразу сохраняется в БД;
- текущий пользователь подсвечивается;
- верхний переключатель пользователей только выбирает пользователя и показывает его цвет.

Но я бы сделал палитру **не фиксированной на 10 цветов**, а автоматически по числу активных пользователей: если пользователей 10 — 10 кружков; добавится 11-й — появится 11-й цвет. Произвольный цвет всегда отдельным кружком.

Это оставляет всю настройку пользователей в техокне, как мы решили.

Да, вижу текущую реализацию. Она уже содержит **32 цвета**, но сейчас палитра фактически используется при добавлении пользователя, а для существующего пользователя нам нужна другая схема.

Сделаем во вкладке **Пользователи**:

- слева — имя пользователя;
- рядом — его текущий цвет;
- далее — палитра **по числу пользователей**;
- последний отдельный кружок — **пипетка произвольного цвета**;
- клик по цвету сразу сохраняет его;
- архивирование — с противоположного края строки;
- верхний переключатель пользователя останется только переключателем;
- выбранный пользователь будет визуально отмечен.

И главное: **32 цвета оставляем источником палитры**, но показываем только столько цветов, сколько сейчас активных пользователей. При 10 пользователях — 10 цветов.

Для этого мне нужен оставшийся кусок файла, потому что вывод оборвался на `color,`. Дай одной командой:

```bash
cd ~/projects/scene_fingerprint_mvp_v02 && \
sed -n '220,420p' app/web/components/technical_modules/users.py
```

После этого дам **одну команду целиком**, которая заменит текущий блок без ручного редактирования.

```bash
def build_users():

    show_archived = {
        "value": False
    }

    root = ui.column().classes(
        "w-full gap-4 p-3"
    )

    with root:

        ui.label(
            "Пользователи"
        ).classes(
            "text-xl font-bold"
        )

        ui.label(
            "Настройка пользователей и цвета интерфейса"
        ).classes(
            "text-sm text-grey-6"
        )

        ui.separator()

        # ====================================================
        # TOP CONTROLS
        # ====================================================

        with ui.row().classes(
            "w-full items-center justify-between"
        ):

            ui.button(
                "Добавить пользователя",
                icon="person_add",
                on_click=lambda: add_user_dialog(),
            ).props(
                "flat dense"
            )

            with ui.row().classes(
                "items-center gap-2"
            ):

                archive_checkbox = ui.checkbox(
                    "Показывать архив",
                    value=False,
                    on_change=lambda e:
                        toggle_archived(
                            e.value
                        ),
                ).props(
                    "dense"
                )

        ui.separator()

        user_list = ui.column().classes(
            "w-full gap-2"
        )

        # ====================================================
        # COLOR HELPERS
        # ====================================================

        def apply_color(
            user_id,
            color,
        ):

            if not color:
                return

            update_user_color(
                user_id,
                color,
            )

            if STATE.user_id == user_id:

                ui.colors(
                    primary=color
                )

                print(
                    "[USER COLOR CHANGED]",
                    user_id,
                    color,
                    flush=True,
                )

            rebuild()

        # ====================================================
        # ADD USER
        # ====================================================

        def add_user_dialog():

            with ui.dialog() as dialog:

                with ui.card().classes(
                    "w-[500px] max-w-[92vw]"
                ):

                    ui.label(
                        "Добавить пользователя"
                    ).classes(
                        "text-lg font-bold"
                    )

                    name_input = ui.input(
                        "Имя пользователя"
                    ).classes(
                        "w-full"
                    )

                    color_state = {
                        "value": USER_COLORS[0]
                    }

                    ui.label(
                        "Цвет пользователя"
                    ).classes(
                        "text-sm font-medium mt-2"
                    )

                    with ui.row().classes(
                        "flex-wrap gap-2"
                    ):

                        for color in USER_COLORS:

                            ui.button(
                                "",
                                on_click=lambda e,
                                c=color:
                                    choose_add_color(
                                        c
                                    ),
                            ).props(
                                "round"
                            ).style(
                                f"""
                                background:{color} !important;
                                width:28px;
                                height:28px;
                                min-width:28px;
                                """
                            )

                    custom_color = ui.color_input(
                        value=color_state["value"]
                    ).classes(
                        "w-full mt-2"
                    )

                    def choose_add_color(color):

                        color_state["value"] = color
                        custom_color.value = color

                    def save():

                        name = (
                            name_input.value
                            or ""
                        ).strip()

                        color = (
                            custom_color.value
                            or color_state["value"]
                        )

                        if not name:

                            ui.notify(
                                "Введите имя пользователя",
                                type="warning",
                            )

                            return

                        if not add_user(
                            name,
                            color,
                        ):

                            ui.notify(
                                "Такой пользователь уже существует",
                                type="warning",
                            )

                            return

                        dialog.close()
                        rebuild()

                    with ui.row().classes(
                        "w-full justify-end gap-2 mt-3"
                    ):

                        ui.button(
                            "Отмена",
                            on_click=dialog.close,
                        ).props(
                            "flat"
                        )

                        ui.button(
                            "Добавить",
                            icon="person_add",
                            on_click=save,
                        )

            dialog.open()

        # ====================================================
        # ARCHIVE
        # ====================================================

        def toggle_archive(
            user_id,
            active,
        ):

            if active:

                if STATE.user_id == user_id:

                    ui.notify(
                        "Нельзя архивировать текущего пользователя",
                        type="warning",
                    )

                    return

                archive_user(
                    user_id
                )

            else:

                restore_user(
                    user_id
                )

            rebuild()

        # ====================================================
        # SHOW ARCHIVED
        # ====================================================

        def toggle_archived(value):

            show_archived["value"] = bool(value)

            rebuild()

        # ====================================================
        # REBUILD
        # ====================================================

        def rebuild():

            user_list.clear()

            users = get_users(
                active_only=not show_archived["value"]
            )

            active_users = [
                user
                for user in users
                if user[3]
            ]

            palette_count = min(
                len(active_users),
                len(USER_COLORS),
            )

            with user_list:

                if not users:

                    ui.label(
                        "Нет пользователей"
                    ).classes(
                        "text-grey-6 p-4"
                    )

                    return

                for index, (
                    user_id,
                    name,
                    color,
                    active,
                ) in enumerate(users):

                    is_current = (
                        STATE.user_id == user_id
                    )

                    with ui.card().classes(
                        "w-full"
                    ):

                        # ====================================
                        # USER ROW
                        # ====================================

                        with ui.row().classes(
                            "w-full items-center gap-3"
                        ):

                            # COLOR INDICATOR

                            ui.element(
                                "div"
                            ).style(
                                f"""
                                width:18px;
                                height:42px;
                                min-width:18px;
                                border-radius:4px;
                                background:{color};
                                """
                            )

                            # NAME

                            with ui.column().classes(
                                "flex-grow gap-0"
                            ):

                                ui.label(
                                    name
                                ).classes(
                                    "font-medium"
                                )

                                if is_current:

                                    ui.label(
                                        "Текущий пользователь"
                                    ).classes(
                                        "text-xs text-primary"
                                    )

                                if not active:

                                    ui.label(
                                        "Архив"
                                    ).classes(
                                        "text-xs text-grey-6"
                                    )

                            # ARCHIVE / RESTORE
                            # Справа

                            ui.button(
                                icon=(
                                    "archive"
                                    if active
                                    else "unarchive"
                                ),
                                on_click=lambda e,
                                uid=user_id,
                                a=active:
                                    toggle_archive(
                                        uid,
                                        a,
                                    ),
                            ).props(
                                "flat round dense"
                            )

                        # ====================================
                        # PALETTE
                        # ====================================

                        if active:

                            with ui.row().classes(
                                "w-full items-center "
                                "gap-2 mt-2"
                            ):

                                # --------------------------------
                                # Палитра по числу пользователей
                                # --------------------------------

                                for palette_color in USER_COLORS[
                                    :palette_count
                                ]:

                                    is_selected = (
                                        color.upper()
                                        == palette_color.upper()
                                    )

                                    ui.button(
                                        "",
                                        on_click=lambda e,
                                        uid=user_id,
                                        c=palette_color:
                                            apply_color(
                                                uid,
                                                c,
                                            ),
                                    ).props(
                                        "round"
                                    ).style(
                                        f"""
                                        background:{palette_color}
                                        !important;
                                        width:30px;
                                        height:30px;
                                        min-width:30px;
                                        border:2px solid
                                        {
                                            '#FFFFFF'
                                            if is_selected
                                            else 'transparent'
                                        };
                                        box-shadow:
                                        {
                                            '0 0 0 2px #555'
                                            if is_selected
                                            else 'none'
                                        };
                                        """
                                    )

                                # --------------------------------
                                # Произвольный цвет / ПИПЕТКА
                                # --------------------------------

                                custom_color = ui.color_input(
                                    value=color,
                                ).props(
                                    "round"
                                ).classes(
                                    "ml-2"
                                )

                                custom_color.on(
                                    "update:model-value",
                                    lambda e,
                                    uid=user_id:
                                        apply_color(
                                            uid,
                                            e.args
                                            if isinstance(
                                                e.args,
                                                str
                                            )
                                            else e.value,
                                        ),
                                )

        rebuild()
```

### Что должно получиться

В техническом разделе → **Пользователи**:

```text
Вихляева Алена                         [архив]
●  ● ● ● ● ● ● ● ● ●  [пипетка]

Пятницына Диана                        [архив]
●  ● ● ● ● ● ● ● ● ●  [пипетка]

...
```

При 10 активных пользователях будет **10 цветов** из общей 32-цветной палитры. Если позже пользователей станет 15 - автоматически будет 15 цветов.

**Пипетка** остаётся отдельным элементом и позволяет выбрать любой цвет.

А верхняя кнопка **«Пользователь»** при этом продолжает только переключать пользователя; его цвет берётся из БД и применяется к интерфейсу.

## Response:
8/16/2026, 8:29:42 PM

Да. Лучше сделать **редактирование имени прямо в строке пользователя**, без отдельного окна.

Предлагаю компактную строку:

`●  Имя пользователя   [цвет] [✎] [архив]`

При нажатии ✎ имя превращается в поле ввода + появляются **✓ / ✕**.  
При сохранении меняем имя в БД, при этом `STATE.user_id` остаётся тем же.

Для этого в `users_db.py` добавим:

```python
def update_user_name(user_id, name):

    name = str(name).strip()

    if not name:
        return False

    init_users_db()

    try:
        with get_connection() as con:
            con.execute(
                """
                UPDATE users
                SET name = ?
                WHERE id = ?
                """,
                (name, user_id),
            )
            con.commit()

        return True

    except sqlite3.IntegrityError:
        return False
```

И в `users.py` импортируем:

```python
from app.web.users_db import (
    get_users,
    add_user,
    update_user_name,
    update_user_color,
    archive_user,
    restore_user,
)
```

А в `rebuild()` вместо обычного `ui.label(name)` сделаем компактное редактирование.

Если хочешь, следующим сообщением дам **одну готовую команду**, которая полностью заменит `users.py` и добавит редактирование имени + компактный интерфейс, не трогая остальную систему.

### 1. Сначала добавь функцию в `users_db.py`

```python
def update_user_name(user_id, name):

    name = str(name).strip()

    if not name:
        return False

    init_users_db()

    try:
        with get_connection() as con:
            con.execute(
                """
                UPDATE users
                SET name = ?
                WHERE id = ?
                """,
                (name, user_id),
            )
            con.commit()

        return True

    except sqlite3.IntegrityError:
        return False
```

### 2. Полностью замени `users.py`

```python
from nicegui import ui

from app.web.state import STATE
from app.web.users_db import (
    get_users,
    add_user,
    update_user_name,
    update_user_color,
    archive_user,
    restore_user,
)

# ============================================================
# PALETTE
# ============================================================

USER_COLORS = [
    "#E53935",
    "#D81B60",
    "#8E24AA",
    "#5E35B1",
    "#3949AB",
    "#1E88E5",
    "#039BE5",
    "#00ACC1",
    "#00897B",
    "#43A047",
    "#7CB342",
    "#C0CA33",
    "#FDD835",
    "#FFB300",
    "#FB8C00",
    "#F4511E",
    "#6D4C41",
    "#757575",
    "#546E7A",
    "#263238",
    "#AD1457",
    "#6A1B9A",
    "#283593",
    "#1565C0",
    "#00695C",
    "#2E7D32",
    "#558B2F",
    "#9E9D24",
    "#EF6C00",
    "#BF360C",
    "#4E342E",
    "#37474F",
]

# ============================================================
# USERS
# ============================================================

def build_users():

    show_archived = {
        "value": False,
    }

    root = ui.column().classes(
        "w-full gap-2 p-2"
    )

    # ========================================================
    # HEADER
    # ========================================================

    with root:

        with ui.row().classes(
            "w-full items-center justify-between"
        ):

            with ui.column().classes("gap-0"):

                ui.label(
                    "Пользователи"
                ).classes(
                    "text-lg font-bold"
                )

                ui.label(
                    "Имя и цвет интерфейса"
                ).classes(
                    "text-xs text-grey-6"
                )

            with ui.row().classes(
                "items-center gap-1"
            ):

                ui.checkbox(
                    "Архив",
                    value=False,
                    on_change=lambda e:
                        toggle_archived(e.value),
                ).props(
                    "dense"
                )

                ui.button(
                    icon="person_add",
                    on_click=lambda: add_user_dialog(),
                ).props(
                    "flat round dense"
                )

        ui.separator()

        user_list = ui.column().classes(
            "w-full gap-1"
        )

    # ========================================================
    # ARCHIVE FILTER
    # ========================================================

    def toggle_archived(value):

        show_archived["value"] = bool(value)

        rebuild()

    # ========================================================
    # CURRENT USER COLOR
    # ========================================================

    def apply_user_color(color):

        if not color:
            return

        ui.colors(
            primary=color
        )

        print(
            "[USER COLOR]",
            color,
            flush=True,
        )

    # ========================================================
    # SELECT USER
    # ========================================================

    def select_user(
        user_id,
        name,
        color,
    ):

        STATE.user_id = user_id
        STATE.manager = name

        apply_user_color(color)

        print(
            "[USER SELECT]",
            user_id,
            repr(name),
            color,
            flush=True,
        )

        rebuild()

    # ========================================================
    # CHANGE COLOR
    # ========================================================

    def choose_existing_color(
        user_id,
        color,
    ):

        if not color:
            return

        update_user_color(
            user_id,
            color,
        )

        if STATE.user_id == user_id:

            apply_user_color(
                color
            )

        rebuild()

    # ========================================================
    # ARCHIVE / RESTORE
    # ========================================================

    def toggle_archive(
        user_id,
        active,
    ):

        if active:

            if STATE.user_id == user_id:

                ui.notify(
                    "Нельзя архивировать текущего пользователя",
                    type="warning",
                )

                return

            archive_user(
                user_id
            )

        else:

            restore_user(
                user_id
            )

        rebuild()

    # ========================================================
    # EDIT NAME
    # ========================================================

    def edit_user_name(
        user_id,
        old_name,
    ):

        with ui.dialog() as dialog:

            with ui.card().classes(
                "w-[400px] max-w-[90vw]"
            ):

                ui.label(
                    "Редактирование пользователя"
                ).classes(
                    "text-lg font-bold"
                )

                name_input = ui.input(
                    "Имя",
                    value=old_name,
                ).props(
                    "autofocus"
                ).classes(
                    "w-full"
                )

                def save_name():

                    new_name = (
                        name_input.value
                        or ""
                    ).strip()

                    if not new_name:

                        ui.notify(
                            "Имя не может быть пустым",
                            type="warning",
                        )

                        return

                    if new_name == old_name:

                        dialog.close()
                        return

                    if not update_user_name(
                        user_id,
                        new_name,
                    ):

                        ui.notify(
                            "Такой пользователь уже существует",
                            type="warning",
                        )

                        return

                    if STATE.user_id == user_id:

                        STATE.manager = new_name

                    print(
                        "[USER NAME CHANGED]",
                        user_id,
                        repr(old_name),
                        "->",
                        repr(new_name),
                        flush=True,
                    )

                    dialog.close()

                    rebuild()

                with ui.row().classes(
                    "w-full justify-end gap-1 mt-2"
                ):

                    ui.button(
                        "Отмена",
                        on_click=dialog.close,
                    ).props(
                        "flat"
                    )

                    ui.button(
                        "Сохранить",
                        icon="check",
                        on_click=save_name,
                    )

        dialog.open()

    # ========================================================
    # ADD USER
    # ========================================================

    def add_user_dialog():

        color_state = {
            "value": USER_COLORS[5],
        }

        with ui.dialog() as dialog:

            with ui.card().classes(
                "w-[440px] max-w-[92vw]"
            ):

                ui.label(
                    "Добавить пользователя"
                ).classes(
                    "text-lg font-bold"
                )

                name_input = ui.input(
                    "Имя пользователя"
                ).classes(
                    "w-full"
                )

                ui.label(
                    "Цвет"
                ).classes(
                    "text-sm text-grey-7"
                )

                color_preview = ui.element(
                    "div"
                ).classes(
                    "w-10 h-10 rounded-full"
                ).style(
                    f"background: {color_state['value']};"
                )

                # --------------------------------------------
                # PALETTE
                # --------------------------------------------

                with ui.row().classes(
                    "flex-wrap gap-1"
                ):

                    for color in USER_COLORS:

                        ui.button(
                            "",
                            on_click=lambda e,
                            c=color:
                                choose_add_color(
                                    c,
                                    color_state,
                                    color_preview,
                                ),
                        ).props(
                            "round"
                        ).style(
                            f"""
                            background:{color} !important;
                            width:26px;
                            height:26px;
                            min-width:26px;
                            """
                        )

                custom_color = ui.color_input(
                    value=color_state["value"]
                ).classes(
                    "w-full"
                )

                # --------------------------------------------
                # SAVE
                # --------------------------------------------

                def save():

                    name = (
                        name_input.value
                        or ""
                    ).strip()

                    color = (
                        custom_color.value
                        or color_state["value"]
                    )

                    if not name:

                        ui.notify(
                            "Введите имя пользователя",
                            type="warning",
                        )

                        return

                    if not add_user(
                        name,
                        color,
                    ):

                        ui.notify(
                            "Такой пользователь уже существует",
                            type="warning",
                        )

                        return

                    dialog.close()

                    rebuild()

                with ui.row().classes(
                    "w-full justify-end gap-1 mt-2"
                ):

                    ui.button(
                        "Отмена",
                        on_click=dialog.close,
                    ).props(
                        "flat"
                    )

                    ui.button(
                        "Добавить",
                        icon="person_add",
                        on_click=save,
                    )

        dialog.open()

    # ========================================================
    # ADD COLOR
    # ========================================================

    def choose_add_color(
        color,
        state,
        preview,
    ):

        state["value"] = color

        preview.style(
            f"background:{color};"
        )

    # ========================================================
    # REBUILD
    # ========================================================

    def rebuild():

        user_list.clear()

        with user_list:

            users = get_users(
                active_only=not show_archived["value"]
            )

            if not users:

                ui.label(
                    "Нет пользователей"
                ).classes(
                    "text-grey-6 p-3"
                )

                return

            for (
                user_id,
                name,
                color,
                active,
            ) in users:

                is_current = (
                    STATE.user_id == user_id
                )

                # ============================================
                # USER ROW
                # ============================================

                with ui.card().classes(
                    "w-full p-1"
                ):

                    with ui.row().classes(
                        "w-full items-center gap-1"
                    ):

                        # ------------------------------------
                        # COLOR
                        # ------------------------------------

                        ui.element(
                            "div"
                        ).style(
                            f"""
                            width:12px;
                            height:30px;
                            min-width:12px;
                            border-radius:3px;
                            background:{color};
                            """
                        )

                        # ------------------------------------
                        # USER BUTTON
                        # ------------------------------------

                        button = ui.button(
                            name,
                            icon=(
                                "check"
                                if is_current
                                else "person"
                            ),
                            on_click=lambda e,
                            uid=user_id,
                            n=name,
                            c=color:
                                select_user(
                                    uid,
                                    n,
                                    c,
                                ),
                        ).props(
                            "flat dense"
                        ).classes(
                            "flex-grow justify-start text-left"
                        )

                        if is_current:

                            button.classes(
                                "font-bold"
                            )

                        # ------------------------------------
                        # EDIT
                        # ------------------------------------

                        ui.button(
                            icon="edit",
                            on_click=lambda e,
                            uid=user_id,
                            n=name:
                                edit_user_name(
                                    uid,
                                    n,
                                ),
                        ).props(
                            "flat round dense"
                        )

                        # ------------------------------------
                        # ARCHIVE
                        # ------------------------------------

                        ui.button(
                            icon=(
                                "archive"
                                if active
                                else "unarchive"
                            ),
                            on_click=lambda e,
                            uid=user_id,
                            a=active:
                                toggle_archive(
                                    uid,
                                    a,
                                ),
                        ).props(
                            "flat round dense"
                        )

                    # ========================================
                    # COLOR PALETTE
                    # ========================================

                    if active:

                        with ui.row().classes(
                            "flex-wrap gap-1 pl-5 pb-1"
                        ):

                            for palette_color in USER_COLORS:

                                selected = (
                                    str(color).upper()
                                    == palette_color.upper()
                                )

                                ui.button(
                                    "",
                                    on_click=lambda e,
                                    uid=user_id,
                                    c=palette_color:
                                        choose_existing_color(
                                            uid,
                                            c,
                                        ),
                                ).props(
                                    "round"
                                ).style(
                                    f"""
                                    background:{palette_color} !important;
                                    width:20px;
                                    height:20px;
                                    min-width:20px;
                                    border:
                                        {
                                            "3px solid white"
                                            if selected
                                            else "1px solid #ccc"
                                        };
                                    """
                                )

                            # --------------------------------
                            # CUSTOM COLOR
                            # --------------------------------

                            ui.color_input(
                                value=color
                            ).props(
                                "round"
                            ).classes(
                                "w-6 h-6"
                            ).on(
                                "change",
                                lambda e,
                                uid=user_id:
                                    choose_existing_color(
                                        uid,
                                        e.value,
                                    ),
                            )

    # ========================================================
    # INITIAL BUILD
    # ========================================================

    rebuild()
```

```bash
cd ~/projects/scene_fingerprint_mvp_v02 && \
cat > app/web/users_db.py <<'PY'
from pathlib import Path
import sqlite3

from app.web.config import DB_PATH

DEFAULT_USERS = [
    ("Вихляева Алена", "#E53935"),
    ("Пятницына Диана", "#D81B60"),
    ("Аннин Илья", "#8E24AA"),
    ("Иванова Елена", "#5E35B1"),
    ("Марышева Елена", "#3949AB"),
    ("Бутовченко Ольга", "#1E88E5"),
    ("Кривошлыкова Ангелина", "#039BE5"),
    ("Теплинская Наталья", "#00ACC1"),
    ("Папикян Эля", "#00897B"),
    ("Путилина Татьяна", "#43A047"),
]

def get_connection():
    DB_PATH.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    return sqlite3.connect(DB_PATH)

def init_users_db():

    with get_connection() as con:

        con.execute("""
            CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS users (
                id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
                name TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE,
                color TEXT NOT NULL,
                active INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
                created_at TEXT DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
                archived_at TEXT
            )
        """)

        count = con.execute(
            "SELECT COUNT(*) FROM users"
        ).fetchone()[0]

        if count == 0:
            con.executemany(
                """
                INSERT INTO users (name, color, active)
                VALUES (?, ?, 1)
                """,
                DEFAULT_USERS,
            )

        con.commit()

def get_users(active_only=True):

    init_users_db()

    with get_connection() as con:

        if active_only:
            rows = con.execute("""
                SELECT id, name, color, active
                FROM users
                WHERE active = 1
                ORDER BY name COLLATE NOCASE
            """).fetchall()
        else:
            rows = con.execute("""
                SELECT id, name, color, active
                FROM users
                ORDER BY active DESC, name COLLATE NOCASE
            """).fetchall()

    return rows

def add_user(name, color):

    name = str(name).strip()

    if not name:
        return False

    init_users_db()

    try:
        with get_connection() as con:
            con.execute(
                """
                INSERT INTO users (name, color, active)
                VALUES (?, ?, 1)
                """,
                (name, color),
            )
            con.commit()

        return True

    except sqlite3.IntegrityError:
        return False

def update_user_name(user_id, name):

    name = str(name).strip()

    if not name:
        return False

    init_users_db()

    try:
        with get_connection() as con:
            con.execute(
                """
                UPDATE users
                SET name = ?
                WHERE id = ?
                """,
                (name, user_id),
            )
            con.commit()

        return True

    except sqlite3.IntegrityError:
        return False

def update_user_color(user_id, color):

    with get_connection() as con:
        con.execute(
            """
            UPDATE users
            SET color = ?
            WHERE id = ?
            """,
            (color, user_id),
        )
        con.commit()

def archive_user(user_id):

    with get_connection() as con:
        con.execute(
            """
            UPDATE users
            SET active = 0,
                archived_at = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
            WHERE id = ?
            """,
            (user_id,),
        )
        con.commit()

def restore_user(user_id):

    with get_connection() as con:
        con.execute(
            """
            UPDATE users
            SET active = 1,
                archived_at = NULL
            WHERE id = ?
            """,
            (user_id,),
        )
        con.commit()
PY
```

from nicegui import ui

from app.web.state import STATE
from app.web.users_db import (
    get_users,
    add_user,
    update_user_color,
    archive_user,
    restore_user,
)

# ============================================================
# PALETTE — 32 COLORS
# ============================================================

USER_COLORS = [
    "#E53935",
    "#D81B60",
    "#8E24AA",
    "#5E35B1",
    "#3949AB",
    "#1E88E5",
    "#039BE5",
    "#00ACC1",
    "#00897B",
    "#43A047",
    "#7CB342",
    "#C0CA33",
    "#FDD835",
    "#FFB300",
    "#FB8C00",
    "#F4511E",
    "#6D4C41",
    "#757575",
    "#546E7A",
    "#263238",
    "#AD1457",
    "#6A1B9A",
    "#283593",
    "#1565C0",
    "#00695C",
    "#2E7D32",
    "#558B2F",
    "#9E9D24",
    "#EF6C00",
    "#BF360C",
    "#4E342E",
    "#37474F",
]

def build_users():

    show_archived = {
        "value": False
    }

    root = ui.column().classes(
        "w-full gap-4 p-3"
    )

    with root:

        ui.label(
            "Пользователи"
        ).classes(
            "text-xl font-bold"
        )

        ui.label(
            "Настройка пользователей и цвета интерфейса"
        ).classes(
            "text-sm text-grey-6"
        )

        ui.separator()

        # ====================================================
        # TOP CONTROLS
        # ====================================================

        with ui.row().classes(
            "w-full items-center justify-between"
        ):

            ui.button(
                "Добавить пользователя",
                icon="person_add",
                on_click=lambda: add_user_dialog(),
            ).props(
                "flat dense"
            )

            with ui.row().classes(
                "items-center gap-2"
            ):

                archive_checkbox = ui.checkbox(
                    "Показывать архив",
                    value=False,
                    on_change=lambda e:
                        toggle_archived(
                            e.value
                        ),
                ).props(
                    "dense"
                )

        ui.separator()

        user_list = ui.column().classes(
            "w-full gap-2"
        )

        # ====================================================
        # COLOR HELPERS
        # ====================================================

        def apply_color(
            user_id,
            color,
        ):

            if not color:
                return

            update_user_color(
                user_id,
                color,
            )

            if STATE.user_id == user_id:

                ui.colors(
                    primary=color
                )

                print(
                    "[USER COLOR CHANGED]",
                    user_id,
                    color,
                    flush=True,
                )

            rebuild()

        # ====================================================
        # ADD USER
        # ====================================================

        def add_user_dialog():

            with ui.dialog() as dialog:

                with ui.card().classes(
                    "w-[500px] max-w-[92vw]"
                ):

                    ui.label(
                        "Добавить пользователя"
                    ).classes(
                        "text-lg font-bold"
                    )

                    name_input = ui.input(
                        "Имя пользователя"
                    ).classes(
                        "w-full"
                    )

                    color_state = {
                        "value": USER_COLORS[0]
                    }

                    ui.label(
                        "Цвет пользователя"
                    ).classes(
                        "text-sm font-medium mt-2"
                    )

                    with ui.row().classes(
                        "flex-wrap gap-2"
                    ):

                        for color in USER_COLORS:

                            ui.button(
                                "",
                                on_click=lambda e,
                                c=color:
                                    choose_add_color(
                                        c
                                    ),
                            ).props(
                                "round"
                            ).style(
                                f"""
                                background:{color} !important;
                                width:28px;
                                height:28px;
                                min-width:28px;
                                """
                            )

                    custom_color = ui.color_input(
                        value=color_state["value"]
                    ).classes(
                        "w-full mt-2"
                    )

                    def choose_add_color(color):

                        color_state["value"] = color
                        custom_color.value = color

                    def save():

                        name = (
                            name_input.value
                            or ""
                        ).strip()

                        color = (
                            custom_color.value
                            or color_state["value"]
                        )

                        if not name:

                            ui.notify(
                                "Введите имя пользователя",
                                type="warning",
                            )

                            return

                        if not add_user(
                            name,
                            color,
                        ):

                            ui.notify(
                                "Такой пользователь уже существует",
                                type="warning",
                            )

                            return

                        dialog.close()
                        rebuild()

                    with ui.row().classes(
                        "w-full justify-end gap-2 mt-3"
                    ):

                        ui.button(
                            "Отмена",
                            on_click=dialog.close,
                        ).props(
                            "flat"
                        )

                        ui.button(
                            "Добавить",
                            icon="person_add",
                            on_click=save,
                        )

            dialog.open()

        # ====================================================
        # ARCHIVE
        # ====================================================

        def toggle_archive(
            user_id,
            active,
        ):

            if active:

                if STATE.user_id == user_id:

                    ui.notify(
                        "Нельзя архивировать текущего пользователя",
                        type="warning",
                    )

                    return

                archive_user(
                    user_id
                )

            else:

                restore_user(
                    user_id
                )

            rebuild()

        # ====================================================
        # SHOW ARCHIVED
        # ====================================================

        def toggle_archived(value):

            show_archived["value"] = bool(value)

            rebuild()

        # ====================================================
        # REBUILD
        # ====================================================

        def rebuild():

            user_list.clear()

            users = get_users(
                active_only=not show_archived["value"]
            )

            active_users = [
                user
                for user in users
                if user[3]
            ]

            palette_count = min(
                len(active_users),
                len(USER_COLORS),
            )

            with user_list:

                if not users:

                    ui.label(
                        "Нет пользователей"
                    ).classes(
                        "text-grey-6 p-4"
                    )

                    return

                for index, (
                    user_id,
                    name,
                    color,
                    active,
                ) in enumerate(users):

                    is_current = (
                        STATE.user_id == user_id
                    )

                    with ui.card().classes(
                        "w-full"
                    ):

                        # ====================================
                        # USER ROW
                        # ====================================

                        with ui.row().classes(
                            "w-full items-center gap-3"
                        ):

                            # COLOR INDICATOR

                            ui.element(
                                "div"
                            ).style(
                                f"""
                                width:18px;
                                height:42px;
                                min-width:18px;
                                border-radius:4px;
                                background:{color};
                                """
                            )

                            # NAME

                            with ui.column().classes(
                                "flex-grow gap-0"
                            ):

                                ui.label(
                                    name
                                ).classes(
                                    "font-medium"
                                )

                                if is_current:

                                    ui.label(
                                        "Текущий пользователь"
                                    ).classes(
                                        "text-xs text-primary"
                                    )

                                if not active:

                                    ui.label(
                                        "Архив"
                                    ).classes(
                                        "text-xs text-grey-6"
                                    )

                            # ARCHIVE / RESTORE
                            # Справа

                            ui.button(
                                icon=(
                                    "archive"
                                    if active
                                    else "unarchive"
                                ),
                                on_click=lambda e,
                                uid=user_id,
                                a=active:
                                    toggle_archive(
                                        uid,
                                        a,
                                    ),
                            ).props(
                                "flat round dense"
                            )

                        # ====================================
                        # PALETTE
                        # ====================================

                        if active:

                            with ui.row().classes(
                                "w-full items-center "
                                "gap-2 mt-2"
                            ):

                                # --------------------------------
                                # Палитра по числу пользователей
                                # --------------------------------

                                for palette_color in USER_COLORS[
                                    :palette_count
                                ]:

                                    is_selected = (
                                        color.upper()
                                        == palette_color.upper()
                                    )

                                    ui.button(
                                        "",
                                        on_click=lambda e,
                                        uid=user_id,
                                        c=palette_color:
                                            apply_color(
                                                uid,
                                                c,
                                            ),
                                    ).props(
                                        "round"
                                    ).style(
                                        f"""
                                        background:{palette_color}
                                        !important;
                                        width:30px;
                                        height:30px;
                                        min-width:30px;
                                        border:2px solid
                                        {
                                            '#FFFFFF'
                                            if is_selected
                                            else 'transparent'
                                        };
                                        box-shadow:
                                        {
                                            '0 0 0 2px #555'
                                            if is_selected
                                            else 'none'
                                        };
                                        """
                                    )

                                # --------------------------------
                                # Произвольный цвет / ПИПЕТКА
                                # --------------------------------

                                custom_color = ui.color_input(
                                    value=color,
                                ).props(
                                    "round"
                                ).classes(
                                    "ml-2"
                                )

                                custom_color.on(
                                    "update:model-value",
                                    lambda e,
                                    uid=user_id:
                                        apply_color(
                                            uid,
                                            e.args
                                            if isinstance(
                                                e.args,
                                                str
                                            )
                                            else e.value,
                                        ),
                                )

        rebuild()
===== USERS DB =====
from pathlib import Path
import sqlite3

from app.web.config import DB_PATH

DEFAULT_USERS = [
    ("Вихляева Алена", "#E53935"),
    ("Пятницына Диана", "#D81B60"),
    ("Аннин Илья", "#8E24AA"),
    ("Иванова Елена", "#5E35B1"),
    ("Марышева Елена", "#3949AB"),
    ("Бутовченко Ольга", "#1E88E5"),
    ("Кривошлыкова Ангелина", "#039BE5"),
    ("Теплинская Наталья", "#00ACC1"),
    ("Папикян Эля", "#00897B"),
    ("Путилина Татьяна", "#43A047"),
]

def get_connection():
    DB_PATH.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    return sqlite3.connect(DB_PATH)

def init_users_db():

    with get_connection() as con:

        con.execute("""
            CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS users (
                id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
                name TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE,
                color TEXT NOT NULL,
                active INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
                created_at TEXT DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
                archived_at TEXT
            )
        """)

        count = con.execute(
            "SELECT COUNT(*) FROM users"
        ).fetchone()[0]

        if count == 0:
            con.executemany(
                """
                INSERT INTO users (name, color, active)
                VALUES (?, ?, 1)
                """,
                DEFAULT_USERS,
            )

        con.commit()

def get_users(active_only=True):

    init_users_db()

    with get_connection() as con:

        if active_only:
            rows = con.execute("""
                SELECT id, name, color, active
                FROM users
                WHERE active = 1
                ORDER BY name COLLATE NOCASE
            """).fetchall()
        else:
            rows = con.execute("""
                SELECT id, name, color, active
                FROM users
                ORDER BY active DESC, name COLLATE NOCASE
            """).fetchall()

    return rows

def add_user(name, color):

    name = str(name).strip()

    if not name:
        return False

    init_users_db()

    try:
        with get_connection() as con:
            con.execute(
                """
                INSERT INTO users (name, color, active)
                VALUES (?, ?, 1)
                """,
                (name, color),
            )
            con.commit()

        return True

    except sqlite3.IntegrityError:
        return False

def update_user_name(user_id, name):

    name = str(name).strip()

    if not name:
        return False

    init_users_db()

    try:
        with get_connection() as con:
            con.execute(
                """
                UPDATE users
                SET name = ?
                WHERE id = ?
                """,
                (name, user_id),
            )
            con.commit()

        return True

    except sqlite3.IntegrityError:
        return False

def update_user_color(user_id, color):

    with get_connection() as con:
        con.execute(
            """
            UPDATE users
            SET color = ?
            WHERE id = ?
            """,
            (color, user_id),
        )
        con.commit()

def archive_user(user_id):

    with get_connection() as con:
        con.execute(
            """
            UPDATE users
            SET active = 0,
                archived_at = CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
            WHERE id = ?
            """,
            (user_id,),
        )
        con.commit()

def restore_user(user_id):

    with get_connection() as con:
        con.execute(
            """
            UPDATE users
            SET active = 1,
                archived_at = NULL
            WHERE id = ?
            """,
            (user_id,),
        )
        con.commit()
